//! `check`'s advisory notes, ranked. A green check printed nine notes in
//! source order, and builders could not tell an FYI about offline coverage
//! from a page that never completes. Notes stay advisory (they never fail a
//! check); this only orders them, names each group, and shortens repeats.

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Debug)]
pub(crate) enum Tier {
    /// Can cost correctness or completeness: a page that never completes, a
    /// write no caller can pass, a disclosure, a read a device cannot answer.
    Review,
    /// Costs speed, meter headroom or offline coverage.
    Performance,
    /// What a declaration implies; nothing to fix for online use.
    Design,
}

pub(crate) fn tier(code: &str) -> Tier {
    match code {
        "N_IMMUTABLE_PATCH" | "N_PERSONA_PRINCIPAL" | "N_UNREADABLE_PROBE" | "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" | "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT" | "N_REFERENCE_UNCHECKED" | "N_MAINTAIN_AUTHORIZATION" | "N_WRITE_DENIED" | "N_DELETE_RESTRICTED" | "N_DELETE_NEEDS_READ" | "N_PATCH_CLEARS" | "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" | "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE" | "N_PROBE_ONLY_READ" | "N_VACUOUS_RULE" | "N_LOOKUP_HIDDEN" | "N_LOOKUP_NEVER_VISIBLE" | "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" | "N_REQUIRE_REFUSED" | "N_QUERY_FILTERED" | "N_SHAPE_METER" | "N_HELD_UNKNOWN" | "N_SEQUENCE_READ"
        | "N_SWEEP_DEADLINE" | "N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD" | "N_PUBLIC_AGGREGATE_COPY" | "N_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_DISCLOSURE" | "N_MAINTAIN_DELTA"
        | "N_SEARCH_UNSCOPED" | "N_SEARCH_OPEN" | "N_TIMELINE_PROFILE_CAP" | "N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_READ" | "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE" | "N_ARGUMENT_GRANT" | "N_BROAD_READ" | "N_SECURITY_INTENT" => Tier::Review,
        // @ref LLP 3000.036#13.4 — a cursorless `first N` is what the declaration says: the first N rows (wave 3 #7).
        "N_SYNC_ORDER" | "N_NO_HORIZON" | "N_MAINTAIN_SYNCED" | "N_TIMELINE_COMPAT" | "N_NO_CURSOR" => Tier::Design,
        _ => Tier::Performance,
    }
}

/// Security findings that can grant authority stay ahead of repeated advice.
pub(crate) fn summary_order(code:&str)->(Tier,u8){
    (tier(code),match code{
        "N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION"=>0,"N_ROLE_WIDE_READ"=>1,
        "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE"=>3,"N_BROAD_READ"=>4,"N_SECURITY_INTENT"=>5,_=>2,
    })
}

/// The first words of a note's line: what to do about it, or that nothing
/// needs doing (wave 7 #11: "put a one-line verdict on every advisory note").
// @ref LLP 3000.036#17.7 — the verdict leads; the explanation follows it.
pub(crate) fn verdict(code: &str, summary: &str, form: bool) -> &'static str {
    match code {
        "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" if summary.starts_with("persona-only:") => "no action without use identity",
        "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" => "fix: add a production bootstrap writer",
        "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT" => "fix: update the fixture arguments",
        "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE" => "review: complete for the pinned parent audience; other callers may be capped",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if summary.contains("the complete form is to add `cap ") => "fix: add the exact source cap below, or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if summary.contains("no verified complete form for this read") => "review: no verified complete form for this read; expect `capped` and show what arrived",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if form && summary.contains("the closest form is") => "fix: use the closest form below (one arm stays `complete: false`), or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if form => "fix: use the complete form below, or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" => "fix: pin the scope, or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_DELETE_RESTRICTED" => "fix: declare the reference's `on delete`, or guard the delete",
        "N_DELETE_NEEDS_READ" => "review: some deleters get NOT_FOUND",
        "N_REFERENCE_UNCHECKED" => "review: this reference argument has no mandatory target lookup",
        "N_MAINTAIN_AUTHORIZATION" => "review: hidden and absent are different caller outcomes",
        "N_PATCH_CLEARS" => "review: an omitted argument clears this column",
        "N_WRITE_DENIED" if summary.contains("admits only 'service:") => "review: a seat require grants no table authority",
        "N_WRITE_DENIED" => "fix: this write refuses E_RULE for callers you admit",
        "N_REQUIRE_REFUSED" => "review: callers you admit may still get E_RULE",
        "N_QUERY_FILTERED" => "review: this query branch may filter rows under table authority",
        "N_READ_DENIED" => "fix: this read always returns null",
        "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" if summary.contains("get NOT_FOUND") => "review: the hidden lookup runs before this require",
        "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" => "fix: this require cannot see the rows it tests",
        "N_LOOKUP_NEVER_VISIBLE" => "fix: this nullable lookup is always null for the admitted callers",
        "N_LOOKUP_HIDDEN" => "review: a required read gate may hide rows from writers",
        "N_PROBE_ONLY_READ" | "N_PUBLIC_AGGREGATE_COPY" | "N_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_DISCLOSURE" => "review: who can read this",
        "N_VACUOUS_RULE" => "review: who can write this",
        "N_IMMUTABLE_PATCH" => "review: readable conflicts require the original immutable value",
        "N_NO_CURSOR" => "no action for a top-N list",
        _ => match tier(code) {
            Tier::Review => "review",
            Tier::Performance => "no action unless it is slow (`check --cost` measures it)",
            Tier::Design => "no action for an online app",
        },
    }
}

fn heading(tier: Tier) -> &'static str {
    match tier {
        Tier::Review => "review — can cost correctness or completeness:",
        Tier::Performance => "performance — costs speed, meter headroom or offline coverage:",
        Tier::Design => "by design — what a declaration implies; no action needed for online use:",
    }
}

pub(crate) struct Note {
    pub site: String,
    pub code: String,
    pub summary: String,
    /// An expanded compiled repair: its lead line and the code. Compact
    /// completeness notes leave this empty and keep the repair in `--verbose`.
    pub form: Option<(String, String)>,
    /// Three-line default rendering for completeness verdicts. Their full
    /// planner proof and compiled repair remain in `check --verbose`.
    pub compact: Option<[String; 3]>,
}

/// The compiled form leading a note's rewrite: the text before "Otherwise:",
/// when it holds code (a lead line ending in `:` and the declaration under it).
// @ref LLP 3000.036#15.5 — the compiled owner-or-X rewrite prints by default, once per form.
pub(crate) fn compiled_form(code: &str, rewrite: &str) -> Option<(String, String)> {
    if !matches!(code, "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" | "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE") { return None; }
    let head = rewrite.split("\n\nOtherwise:").next()?;
    let (lead, body) = head.split_once('\n')?;
    (lead.ends_with(':') && body.trim_start().starts_with("query ")).then(|| (lead.to_owned(), body.trim_end().to_owned()))
}

/// The ranked block printed after `check: ok`; empty for no notes.
pub(crate) fn render(notes: Vec<Note>) -> Vec<String> {
    if notes.is_empty() { return Vec::new(); }
    // Collapse one code's repeats at one site, keeping first-seen order.
    let mut unique: Vec<(Note, usize)> = Vec::new();
    for note in notes {
        match unique.iter_mut().find(|(seen, _)| seen.site == note.site && seen.code == note.code && seen.summary == note.summary && seen.form == note.form && seen.compact == note.compact) {
            Some((_, count)) => *count += 1,
            None => unique.push((note, 1)),
        }
    }
    let total = unique.len();
    // Rank by tier; within a tier, a code's notes stay together in the order the code first appeared.
    let first_seen = |code: &str, unique: &[(Note, usize)]| unique.iter().position(|(n, _)| n.code == code).unwrap();
    let mut order: Vec<usize> = (0..unique.len()).collect();
    order.sort_by_key(|&i| (tier(&unique[i].0.code), first_seen(&unique[i].0.code, &unique), i));
    let mut lines = vec![format!("{total} advisory note{} — notes never block dev, test or deploy (most consequential first; check --verbose explains each, snapback4 why <CODE> one):",
        if total == 1 { "" } else { "s" })];
    let mut current = None;
    let mut shown_codes = Vec::new();
    let mut shown_forms = Vec::new();
    let mut shown_compacts = Vec::new();
    let mut shown_actions = Vec::new();
    let mut shown_context = Vec::new();
    // @ref LLP 3000.036#16.5 — wave 6 #25: a code's repeats share one line naming each subject.
    let mut repeats: Vec<(String, Vec<String>)> = Vec::new();
    let flush = |lines: &mut Vec<String>, repeats: &mut Vec<(String, Vec<String>)>| {
        for (code, subjects) in repeats.drain(..) {
            lines.push(format!("note: {code} (as above) also for {}", subjects.join("; ")));
        }
    };
    for i in order {
        let (note, count) = &unique[i];
        let group = tier(&note.code);
        if current != Some(group) { flush(&mut lines, &mut repeats); lines.push(heading(group).to_owned()); current = Some(group); }
        if note.code == "N_NO_CURSOR" {
            if !shown_codes.contains(&note.code) {
                let subjects = unique.iter().filter(|(n,_)| n.code == "N_NO_CURSOR").map(|(n,_)|
                    format!("{} ({})", subject(&n.summary), n.site)).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("; ");
                lines.push(format!("note: N_NO_CURSOR: no action for a top-N list. Pages without continuation: {subjects}; if the screen pages, add `c: cursor ?` and `after c`"));
                shown_codes.push(note.code.clone());
            }
            continue;
        }
        let times = if *count > 1 { format!(" (×{count} at this site)") } else { String::new() };
        let new_form = note.form.as_ref().is_some_and(|(_, code)| !shown_forms.contains(code));
        let new_compact = note.compact.as_ref().is_some_and(|parts| !shown_compacts.contains(parts));
        if shown_codes.contains(&note.code) && !new_form && !new_compact {
            // A repeat of a code already explained above: its subject and site, on the code's one line.
            let subject = format!("{} ({}){times}", subject(&note.summary), note.site);
            match repeats.iter_mut().find(|(code, _)| *code == note.code) {
                Some((_, subjects)) => subjects.push(subject),
                None => repeats.push((note.code.clone(), vec![subject])),
            }
            continue;
        }
        flush(&mut lines, &mut repeats);
        if let Some(parts @ [action, impact, audience]) = &note.compact {
            if !shown_actions.contains(action) {
                let mut body = action.lines();
                lines.push(format!("note: {} — {} — {}{times}", note.site, note.code, body.next().unwrap_or("review")));
                lines.extend(body.map(str::to_owned));
                shown_actions.push(action.clone());
            } else {
                lines.push(format!("note: {} — {} — {}: checked rewrite or limitation as above{times}", note.site, note.code, subject(&note.summary)));
            }
            for context in [impact, audience] {
                if !shown_context.contains(&(note.code.clone(), context.clone())) {
                    lines.push(format!("  {context}"));
                    shown_context.push((note.code.clone(), context.clone()));
                }
            }
            shown_compacts.push(parts.clone());
            shown_codes.push(note.code.clone());
            continue;
        }
        if shown_codes.contains(&note.code) {
            // A repeat that carries a form not printed yet keeps its own line, then the form.
            lines.push(format!("note: {} — {}: (as above) {}{times}", note.site, note.code, short(&note.summary)));
        } else {
            let below = note.form.as_ref().is_some_and(|(_, code)| !shown_forms.contains(code));
            let summary = match note.summary.strip_suffix(" (check --verbose)") { Some(text) if below => format!("{text} (below)"), _ => note.summary.clone() };
            lines.push(format!("note: {} — {}: {}. {summary}{times}", note.site, note.code, verdict(&note.code, &note.summary, below)));
            shown_codes.push(note.code.clone());
        }
        if let Some((lead, code)) = &note.form && !shown_forms.contains(code) {
            lines.push(format!("  rewrite: {lead}"));
            lines.extend(code.lines().map(|line| format!("    {line}")));
            shown_forms.push(code.clone());
        }
    }
    flush(&mut lines, &mut repeats);
    // Every advisory line has a direct route to the acceptance recipe.
    for line in &mut lines {
        if let Some(code) = line.split_whitespace().find(|word| word.starts_with("N_")) {
            let code = code.trim_end_matches(':').to_owned();
            line.push_str(&format!(" — snapback4 why {code}"));
        }
    }
    lines
}

/// The operation or table a repeat is about (`query b`), else its short subject.
fn subject(summary: &str) -> String {
    let mut words = summary.splitn(3, ' ');
    match (words.next(), words.next()) {
        (Some(kind @ ("query" | "mutation" | "job" | "action" | "table" | "view")), Some(name)) => format!("{kind} {}", name.trim_end_matches(':')),
        _ => short(summary),
    }
}

/// The subject of a repeated note: its first clause before any quoted source, at most 100 characters.
fn short(summary: &str) -> String {
    let clause = summary.split("; ").next().unwrap_or(summary);
    // The quoted source is above already; the subject before it names the repeat.
    let clause = clause.split_once(" `").map_or(clause, |(subject, _)| subject).trim_end_matches(':');
    match clause.char_indices().nth(100) {
        Some((at, _)) => format!("{}…", &clause[..at]),
        None => clause.to_owned(),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn note(site: &str, code: &str, summary: &str) -> Note { Note { site: site.into(), code: code.into(), summary: summary.into(), form: None, compact: None } }

    #[test]
    fn a_compiled_complete_form_prints_once_per_lead_by_default() {
        let rewrite = "Branch on the grant and pin the owner for everyone else; both pages are complete:\nquery q(c: cursor ?):\n  if exists staff[viewer]:\n    return t last 50 by .at after c\n  return t where .owner = viewer last 50 by .at after c\n\nOtherwise: pin .owner";
        let form = compiled_form("N_NEVER_COMPLETE", rewrite);
        assert_eq!(form.as_ref().map(|(lead, _)| lead.as_str()), Some("Branch on the grant and pin the owner for everyone else; both pages are complete:"));
        assert!(compiled_form("N_NEVER_COMPLETE", "pin .owner with where equalities").is_none());
        assert!(compiled_form("N_SYNC_ORDER", rewrite).is_none());
        let with = |site: &str| Note { form: form.clone(), ..note(site, "N_NEVER_COMPLETE", "query q: never complete") };
        let lines = render(vec![with("schema.q:1:1"), with("schema.q:9:1")]);
        assert_eq!(lines[2..8], [
            "note: schema.q:1:1 — N_NEVER_COMPLETE: fix: use the complete form below, or accept `complete: false`. query q: never complete — snapback4 why N_NEVER_COMPLETE".to_owned(),
            "  rewrite: Branch on the grant and pin the owner for everyone else; both pages are complete:".into(),
            "    query q(c: cursor ?):".into(),
            "      if exists staff[viewer]:".into(),
            "        return t last 50 by .at after c".into(),
            "      return t where .owner = viewer last 50 by .at after c".into(),
        ], "{lines:#?}");
        assert_eq!(lines.len(), 9, "the second note of the same form is the short repeat: {lines:#?}");
        assert_eq!(lines[8], "note: N_NEVER_COMPLETE (as above) also for query q (schema.q:9:1) — snapback4 why N_NEVER_COMPLETE", "{lines:#?}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn distinct_scans_at_one_site_and_distinct_query_repairs_remain_visible() {
        let n = |subject: &str, repair: &str| Note { compact: Some([repair.into(), "impact".into(), "audience".into()]), ..note("s.q:1:1", "N_NEVER_COMPLETE", subject) };
        let lines = render(vec![n("query q: scan a", "rewrite a"), n("query q: scan b", "rewrite b"), n("query r", "rewrite r"), n("query r", "rewrite r")]);
        let output = lines.join("\n");
        for repair in ["rewrite a", "rewrite b", "rewrite r"] { assert_eq!(output.matches(repair).count(), 1, "{output}"); }
        assert!(output.contains("×2 at this site"), "{output}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn identical_actions_keep_different_scan_audiences() {
        let with = |audience: &str| Note { compact: Some(["review query both: missing pin .owner".into(), "same impact".into(), audience.into()]), ..note("s.q:1:1", "N_NEVER_COMPLETE", "query both") };
        let output = render(vec![with("staff are complete (scan tickets)"), with("tutors are complete (scan papers)")]).join("\n");
        assert!(output.contains("staff are complete (scan tickets)"), "{output}");
        assert!(output.contains("tutors are complete (scan papers)"), "{output}");
        assert_eq!(output.matches("same impact").count(), 1, "{output}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn completeness_note_is_three_lines_and_points_to_verbose_detail() {
        let compact = Some([
            "verdict: capped; completeness is not proved".into(),
            "who: no caller group is proved complete by this page".into(),
            "what: pin the scope; check --verbose shows the checked repair".into(),
        ]);
        let lines = render(vec![Note { compact, ..note("schema.q:8:1", "N_NEVER_COMPLETE", "long planner proof") }]);
        assert_eq!(&lines[2..], [
            "note: schema.q:8:1 — N_NEVER_COMPLETE — verdict: capped; completeness is not proved — snapback4 why N_NEVER_COMPLETE",
            "  who: no caller group is proved complete by this page",
            "  what: pin the scope; check --verbose shows the checked repair",
        ]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn notes_rank_by_consequence_and_collapse_repeats() {
        let lines = render(vec![
            note("schema.q:95:1", "N_WIDE_MERGE", "query feed: read cost grows"),
            note("schema.q:71:1", "N_MAINTAIN_SYNCED", "2 synced maintains"),
            note("schema.q:78:10", "N_SYNC_ORDER", "query a reads by .id; the client needs the server"),
            note("schema.q:87:10", "N_SYNC_ORDER", "query b `t first 5 by .at` reads by .at; the client needs the server"),
            note("schema.q:104:1", "N_WRITE_DENIED", "mutation rangers inserts rows no caller may insert"),
            note("schema.q:104:1", "N_WRITE_DENIED", "mutation rangers inserts rows no caller may insert"),
        ]);
        assert!(lines[0].starts_with("5 advisory notes — notes never block"), "{lines:?}");
        let body: Vec<&str> = lines[1..].iter().map(String::as_str).collect();
        assert_eq!(body, [
            "review — can cost correctness or completeness:",
            "note: schema.q:104:1 — N_WRITE_DENIED: fix: this write refuses E_RULE for callers you admit. mutation rangers inserts rows no caller may insert (×2 at this site) — snapback4 why N_WRITE_DENIED",
            "performance — costs speed, meter headroom or offline coverage:",
            "note: schema.q:95:1 — N_WIDE_MERGE: no action unless it is slow (`check --cost` measures it). query feed: read cost grows — snapback4 why N_WIDE_MERGE",
            "by design — what a declaration implies; no action needed for online use:",
            "note: schema.q:71:1 — N_MAINTAIN_SYNCED: no action for an online app. 2 synced maintains — snapback4 why N_MAINTAIN_SYNCED",
            "note: schema.q:78:10 — N_SYNC_ORDER: no action for an online app. query a reads by .id; the client needs the server — snapback4 why N_SYNC_ORDER",
            "note: N_SYNC_ORDER (as above) also for query b (schema.q:87:10) — snapback4 why N_SYNC_ORDER",
        ]);
        assert!(render(Vec::new()).is_empty());
    }
}
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#2 — explicit saved bytes replace only source selection, never activation or store semantics.
use super::{Options, Project};
use std::path::Path;

/// Select one complete saved backend, or the ordinary multi-file project.
/// The vector is retained for both compilation and staging: one read, no swap.
pub(super) fn sources(project: &Project, options: &Options, flag: &str) -> Result<Vec<(String, String)>, String> {
    match options.flags.get(flag) {
        None => project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        Some(file) => {
            let path = Path::new(file);
            if !path.try_exists().map_err(|e|format!("cannot inspect saved source {file}: {e}"))? {return Err(format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved source does not exist"));}
            if path.is_dir() {
                return crate::client_compatibility::sources(path).map_err(|e| e.to_string());
            }
            let name = path.file_name().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).ok_or("saved source needs a UTF-8 file name")?;
            if !name.ends_with(".q") { return Err("saved source must be a complete Quarry .q file".into()); }
            let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|e| if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound {format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved source does not exist")} else {format!("cannot read saved source {file}: {e}")})?;
            if text.trim().is_empty() { return Err(format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved Quarry source is empty")); }
            // A saved complete backend retains the schema namespace regardless of its archive file name.
            Ok(vec![("schema.q".into(), text)])
        }
    }
}

pub(super) fn compile(sources: &[(String, String)], options: &Options, flag: &str) -> snapback4_lang::Bound {
    let mut bound = snapback4_lang::compile(sources);
    if let Some(file) = options.flags.get(flag) {
        for diagnostic in &mut bound.diagnostics { diagnostic.site.file = if Path::new(file).is_dir() { Path::new(file).join(&diagnostic.site.file).to_string_lossy().into_owned() } else { file.clone() }; }
    }
    bound
}

/// A saved file can be used outside a project. Companion code is selected
/// only from an actual current project, never from arbitrary sibling files.
pub(super) fn project(options: &Options, flag: &str) -> Result<Project, String> {
    let cwd = std::env::current_dir().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if let Some(project) = Project::locate(&cwd) { return Ok(project); }
    if options.flags.contains_key(flag) { return Ok(Project { root: cwd }); }
    Err("no snapback/*.q here or above; run `snapback4 init` or select a saved source file".into())
}

/// A private companion build uses the selected schema's generated contracts.
/// Packaging still records the original host installation, never this scratch.
pub(super) struct Candidate { pub project: Project, cleanup: std::path::PathBuf }
impl Drop for Candidate { fn drop(&mut self) { let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.cleanup); } }
impl Candidate {
    pub(super) fn prepare(original: &Project, stage: &Path, bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound) -> Result<Self, String> {
        use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering};
        static SERIAL: AtomicUsize = AtomicUsize::new(0);
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("snapback4-saved-source-{}-{}", std::process::id(), SERIAL.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)));
        std::fs::create_dir(&root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        #[cfg(unix)] snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let mut candidate = Self { project: Project { root: root.clone() }, cleanup: root };
        candidate.project.root = crate::dev_worker::private_scope(original, &candidate.cleanup)?;
        // The worker snapshotter captures the actual import closure and refuses
        // symlinks. Copy those captured bytes, not an unbounded application tree.
        copy_tree(stage, &candidate.project.root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        // Discover the current dependency graph without installing selected
        // contracts in the working project. A failed typecheck still publishes
        // its graph; the copied bytes are checked with selected contracts below.
        if let Some(entry) = crate::dev_worker::source(original) {
            let _ = original.check_entry(&entry);
            crate::dev_worker::package_entry(original, &candidate.project.root)?;
        }
        candidate.project.write_generated_from(bound, original).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if !crate::native::declared(bound).is_empty() && original.native_manifest().exists() {
            copy_tree(&original.native_dir(), &candidate.project.native_dir()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            rebase_tree(&candidate.project.native_dir(), &original.native_dir()).map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native snapshot: {e}"))?;
        }
        crate::dev_worker::link_dependencies(original, &candidate.project.root)?;
        if let Some(entry) = crate::dev_worker::source(&candidate.project) { candidate.project.check_entry(&entry)?; }
        Ok(candidate)
    }
}
fn copy_tree(source: &Path, target: &Path) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    std::fs::create_dir_all(target)?;
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(source)? {
        let entry = entry?;
        if matches!(entry.file_name().to_str(), Some("target" | "native-target" | ".git")) { continue; }
        let kind = entry.file_type()?;
        if kind.is_dir() { copy_tree(&entry.path(), &target.join(entry.file_name()))?; }
        else if kind.is_file() { std::fs::copy(entry.path(), target.join(entry.file_name()))?; }
        else { return Err(std::io::Error::other("saved-source companion snapshot permits regular source files only")); }
    }
    Ok(())
}


#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn dir(label: &str) -> std::path::PathBuf {
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-saved-{}-{label}", std::process::id()));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&root); std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("snapback/generated")).unwrap(); root
    }
    #[test]
    fn archived_names_preserve_operations_and_diagnostics_name_the_selected_input() {
        let root = dir("archived"); let project = Project { root: root.clone() };
        let file = root.join("v1.q");
        std::fs::write(&file, "query alive():\n  return true\n").unwrap();
        let options = Options { positionals: vec![], flags: [("schema".into(), file.to_str().unwrap().into())].into(), json: false };
        let selected = sources(&project, &options, "schema").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(selected[0].0, "schema.q");
        let bound = compile(&selected, &options, "schema");
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty()); assert_eq!(bound.programs[0].program.name, "alive");
        std::fs::write(&file, "not a Quarry backend").unwrap();
        let bound = compile(&sources(&project, &options, "schema").unwrap(), &options, "schema");
        assert!(!bound.diagnostics.is_empty()); assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().all(|d| d.site.file == file.to_str().unwrap()));
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn missing_baseline_names_original_absolute_location() {
        let root = dir("missing-baseline"); let project = Project { root: root.clone() };
        std::fs::write(root.join("snapback/client-compatibility.json"), r#"{"baseline":"versions/v1","since_release":2}"#).unwrap();
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), "query alive():\n  return true\n".into())]);
        let error = project.write_generated(&bound).unwrap_err().to_string();
        assert!(error.contains(root.join("versions/v1").to_str().unwrap()) && error.contains("does not exist (resolved from") && error.contains("snapback/client-compatibility.json"), "{error}");
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn inherited_native_workspace_refuses_before_touching_working_generated_types() {
        let root = dir("workspace"); let stage = dir("workspace-stage");
        let original = Project { root: root.clone() };
        std::fs::create_dir_all(original.native_dir()).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(original.native_manifest(), "[package]\nname = \"fixture\"\nversion.workspace = true\n").unwrap();
        std::fs::write(original.native_types(), "working sentinel").unwrap();
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), "job sweep(): native\n".into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let error = Candidate::prepare(&original, &stage, &bound).err().unwrap();
        assert!(error.contains("workspace-inherited") && error.contains("standalone"), "{error}");
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(original.native_types()).unwrap(), "working sentinel");
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap(); std::fs::remove_dir_all(stage).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn native_types_are_built_for_selected_source_in_private_tree() {
        let root = dir("native"); let stage = dir("stage");
        let original = Project { root: root.clone() };
        std::fs::create_dir_all(original.native_dir().join("src")).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(original.native_manifest(), "[package]\nname = \"fixture\"\nversion = \"0.0.0\"\n").unwrap();
        std::fs::write(original.native_types(), "working sentinel").unwrap();
        let source = "table notes:\n  title: text <=80\n  color: text <=20\n  public 'shared'\njob sweep(): native\n";
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("v2.q".into(), source.into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let candidate = Candidate::prepare(&original, &stage, &bound).unwrap();
        crate::native::build(&candidate.project, &bound).err().expect("private build refuses absent native target"); // no lib target: a private build refusal, never working generated writes
        assert!(std::fs::read_to_string(candidate.project.native_types()).unwrap().contains("color"));
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(original.native_types()).unwrap(), "working sentinel");
        let manifest = "[dependencies]\nsdk = { path = \"../sdk\" }\n";
        std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("snapback/sdk")).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(original.native_manifest(), manifest).unwrap();
        let relocated = Candidate::prepare(&original, &stage, &bound).unwrap();
        let parsed: toml::Value = std::fs::read_to_string(relocated.project.native_manifest()).unwrap().parse().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(parsed["dependencies"]["sdk"]["path"].as_str().unwrap(), root.join("snapback/sdk").canonicalize().unwrap().to_str().unwrap());
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(original.native_manifest()).unwrap(), manifest);
        drop(relocated);
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(original.native_types()).unwrap(), "working sentinel");
        drop(candidate); std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap(); std::fs::remove_dir_all(stage).unwrap();
    }
}

fn rebase_native(text: &str, original: &Path, source_root: &Path, target_root: &Path, workspace: bool) -> Result<String, String> {
    let mut manifest: toml::Value = text.parse().map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native manifest: {e}"))?;
    fn inherited(value: &toml::Value) -> bool {
        let marker = |field: &toml::Value| field.get("workspace").and_then(toml::Value::as_bool) == Some(true);
        if value.get("package").and_then(toml::Value::as_table).is_some_and(|fields| fields.iter().any(|(key, field)| key != "metadata" && marker(field))) { return true; }
        for key in ["dependencies", "dev-dependencies", "build-dependencies"] {
            if value.get(key).and_then(toml::Value::as_table).is_some_and(|fields| fields.values().any(marker)) { return true; }
        }
        value.get("target").and_then(toml::Value::as_table).is_some_and(|targets| targets.values().any(inherited))
    }
    if !workspace && inherited(&manifest) { return Err("workspace-inherited native packages require a complete standalone [workspace] under snapback/native; copy its workspace package/dependency declarations before saved-source deployment".into()); }
    fn dependency_group(group: &mut toml::Value, root: &Path, source_root: &Path, target_root: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
        if let Some(dependencies) = group.as_table_mut() {
            for (_, dependency) in dependencies.iter_mut() {
                if let Some(path) = dependency.as_table_mut().and_then(|fields| fields.get_mut("path")) {
                    let relative = path.as_str().ok_or("native dependency path must be a string")?;
                    let absolute = root.join(relative).canonicalize().map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native dependency {relative}: {e}"))?;
                    let absolute = absolute.strip_prefix(source_root).map(|relative| target_root.join(relative)).unwrap_or(absolute.clone());
                    *path = toml::Value::String(absolute.to_str().ok_or("native dependency path must be UTF-8")?.to_owned());
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn groups(table: &mut toml::Value, root: &Path, source_root: &Path, target_root: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
        for key in ["dependencies", "dev-dependencies", "build-dependencies", "replace"] {
            if let Some(group) = table.get_mut(key) { dependency_group(group, root, source_root, target_root)?; }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    groups(&mut manifest, original, source_root, target_root)?;
    for category in ["target", "patch"] {
        if let Some(table) = manifest.get_mut(category).and_then(toml::Value::as_table_mut) {
            for (_, item) in table.iter_mut() {
                if category == "target" { groups(item, original, source_root, target_root)?; }
                else { dependency_group(item, original, source_root, target_root)?; }
            }
        }
    }
    if let Some(workspace) = manifest.get_mut("workspace") { groups(workspace, original, source_root, target_root)?; }
    toml::to_string(&manifest).map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native manifest: {e}"))
}

fn rebase_tree(target: &Path, original: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(target.join("Cargo.toml")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let manifest: toml::Value = text.parse().map_err(|e| format!("native manifest: {e}"))?;
    let workspace = manifest.get("workspace").is_some();
    fn walk(directory: &Path, source_root: &Path, target_root: &Path, workspace: bool) -> Result<(), String> {
        for entry in std::fs::read_dir(directory).map_err(|e| e.to_string())? {
            let entry = entry.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            if entry.file_type().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_dir() { walk(&entry.path(), source_root, target_root, workspace)?; }
            else if entry.file_name() == "Cargo.toml" {
                let relative = entry.path().strip_prefix(target_root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.to_owned();
                let source = source_root.join(relative).parent().unwrap().to_owned();
                let text = std::fs::read_to_string(entry.path()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                let rebased = rebase_native(&text, &source, source_root, target_root, workspace)?;
                std::fs::write(entry.path(), rebased).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    let original = original.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    walk(target, &original, target, workspace)
}
//! Saved-source fixture selection, business cases and the query sweep.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#9-wave-13-saved-version-proofs-day3-2026-10-16 — contemporary fixtures belong to the selected saved source.
use super::*;

const CASE_SUMMARY: &str = "SNAPBACK4_TEST_CASES";

fn case_count(path: &std::path::Path) -> Result<usize, String> {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read {}: {error}", path.display()))?;
    Ok(text.lines().filter(|line| {
        let line = line.trim();
        !line.is_empty() && !line.starts_with("//")
    }).count())
}

/// `test`: run every argument-free query as each persona against the store
/// (optionally loaded from a fixture) and print the meters beside the
/// ceilings, so a builder reads numbers instead of being refused on a bound.
pub fn test(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    let project = match proof_source::project(options, "source") { Ok(project) => project, Err(e) => { eprintln!("test: {e}"); return 2; } };
    let sources = match proof_source::sources(&project, options, "source") { Ok(sources) => sources, Err(e) => { eprintln!("test: {e}"); return 2; } };
    let bound = compiled_or_exit(proof_source::compile(&sources, options, "source"), options.json);
    let mut defaults = options.clone();
    let mut skipped_business_cases = 0;
    if !defaults.flags.contains_key("fixture") {
        let saved = options.flags.get("source").map(std::path::Path::new).map(|path| {
            if path.is_dir() { path.join("fixtures") }
            else if path.file_name().is_some_and(|name| name == "schema.q") { path.parent().unwrap().join("fixtures") }
            else { path.with_file_name(format!("{}-fixtures", path.file_stem().unwrap_or_default().to_string_lossy())) }
        });
        if let Some(fixture) = saved.filter(|path| path.is_dir()) {
            eprintln!("test: using {} (saved version's contemporary fixtures; override with --fixture <dir>)", fixture.display());
            defaults.flags.insert("fixture".into(), fixture.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
        } else if project.root.join("fixtures").is_dir() {
            let fixture = project.root.join("fixtures");
            if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
                defaults.flags.insert("saved-fixture-fallback".into(), String::new());
                eprintln!("test: no contemporary fixtures for saved source; validating only declared-table files in {}; pass --fixture <dir> for a complete contemporary case suite", fixture.display());
                let cases = fixture.join(test_cases::FILE);
                if cases.is_file() {
                    skipped_business_cases = match case_count(&cases) { Ok(count) => count, Err(error) => { eprintln!("test: {error}"); return 1; } };
                    eprintln!("test: skipped {} — working cases may target another release; pass --fixture <dir> to run explicitly", cases.display());
                }
            } else { eprintln!("test: using {} (default fixture directory; override with --fixture <dir>)", fixture.display()); }
            defaults.flags.insert("fixture".into(), fixture.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
        } else if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
            eprintln!("test: no saved or working fixtures; query sweep only; pass --fixture <dir> for business cases");
        }
    }
    let evidence_options=defaults.clone();
    let fixture_inputs=match defaults.flags.get("fixture").filter(|_|!options.flags.contains_key("residency")){
        Some(path)=>match test_fixture::snapshot(std::path::Path::new(path)){Ok(snapshot)=>Some(snapshot),Err(why)=>{eprintln!("test fixture snapshot: {why}");return 1;}},None=>None,
    };
    if let Some(snapshot)=&fixture_inputs {defaults.flags.insert("fixture-input-label".into(),defaults.flags["fixture"].clone());defaults.flags.insert("fixture".into(),snapshot.0.to_string_lossy().into_owned());}
    let consumed=if options.flags.contains_key("residency"){String::new()}else{match security::capture(&sources,fixture_inputs.as_ref().map(|s|s.0.as_path())){Ok(key)=>key,Err(why)=>{eprintln!("test input fingerprint: {why}");return 1;}}};
    let options = &defaults;
    // test applies dev's cast: a persona dev would refuse is not run as an ordinary viewer.
    // Residency measures any principal in the data (scale fixtures name thousands), so it notes instead.
    if let Some(why) = options.flags.get("as").and_then(|names| names.split(',').find_map(|name| test_cases::undeclared_persona(name, &bound.personas))) {
        if !options.flags.contains_key("residency") {
            eprintln!("test --as: {why}");
            return 1;
        }
        eprintln!("test --residency --as: measuring an undeclared principal; dev would refuse it as a persona");
    }
    let _owner = if options.flags.contains_key("fixture") || options.flags.contains_key("source") || !options.flags.contains_key("residency") { None } else {
        match crate::store_owner::command(&project.store_path()) {
            Ok(owner) => Some(owner),
            Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
        }
    };
    let fixture_now = snapback4_core::now_ms();
    let opened = match &_owner {
        Some(owner) => owner.open_engine(&bound, std::time::Duration::from_secs(5), fixture_now).map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        None if !options.flags.contains_key("fixture")&&!options.flags.contains_key("residency") => {

//! `check`'s advisory notes, ranked. A green check printed nine notes in
//! source order, and builders could not tell an FYI about offline coverage
//! from a page that never completes. Notes stay advisory (they never fail a
//! check); this only orders them, names each group, and shortens repeats.

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Debug)]
pub(crate) enum Tier {
    /// Can cost correctness or completeness: a page that never completes, a
    /// write no caller can pass, a disclosure, a read a device cannot answer.
    Review,
    /// Costs speed, meter headroom or offline coverage.
    Performance,
    /// What a declaration implies; nothing to fix for online use.
    Design,
}

pub(crate) fn tier(code: &str) -> Tier {
    match code {
        "N_IMMUTABLE_PATCH" | "N_PERSONA_PRINCIPAL" | "N_UNREADABLE_PROBE" | "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" | "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT" | "N_REFERENCE_UNCHECKED" | "N_MAINTAIN_AUTHORIZATION" | "N_WRITE_DENIED" | "N_DELETE_RESTRICTED" | "N_DELETE_NEEDS_READ" | "N_PATCH_CLEARS" | "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" | "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE" | "N_PROBE_ONLY_READ" | "N_VACUOUS_RULE" | "N_LOOKUP_HIDDEN" | "N_LOOKUP_NEVER_VISIBLE" | "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" | "N_REQUIRE_REFUSED" | "N_QUERY_FILTERED" | "N_SHAPE_METER" | "N_HELD_UNKNOWN" | "N_SEQUENCE_READ"
        | "N_SWEEP_DEADLINE" | "N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD" | "N_PUBLIC_AGGREGATE_COPY" | "N_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_DISCLOSURE" | "N_MAINTAIN_DELTA"
        | "N_SEARCH_UNSCOPED" | "N_SEARCH_OPEN" | "N_TIMELINE_PROFILE_CAP" | "N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_READ" | "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE" | "N_ARGUMENT_GRANT" | "N_BROAD_READ" | "N_SECURITY_INTENT" => Tier::Review,
        // @ref LLP 3000.036#13.4 — a cursorless `first N` is what the declaration says: the first N rows (wave 3 #7).
        "N_SYNC_ORDER" | "N_NO_HORIZON" | "N_MAINTAIN_SYNCED" | "N_TIMELINE_COMPAT" | "N_NO_CURSOR" => Tier::Design,
        _ => Tier::Performance,
    }
}

/// Security findings that can grant authority stay ahead of repeated advice.
pub(crate) fn summary_order(code:&str)->(Tier,u8){
    (tier(code),match code{
        "N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION"=>0,"N_ROLE_WIDE_READ"=>1,
        "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE"=>3,"N_BROAD_READ"=>4,"N_SECURITY_INTENT"=>5,_=>2,
    })
}

/// The first words of a note's line: what to do about it, or that nothing
/// needs doing (wave 7 #11: "put a one-line verdict on every advisory note").
// @ref LLP 3000.036#17.7 — the verdict leads; the explanation follows it.
pub(crate) fn verdict(code: &str, summary: &str, form: bool) -> &'static str {
    match code {
        "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" if summary.starts_with("persona-only:") => "no action without use identity",
        "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" => "fix: add a production bootstrap writer",
        "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT" => "fix: update the fixture arguments",
        "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE" => "review: complete for the pinned parent audience; other callers may be capped",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if summary.contains("the complete form is to add `cap ") => "fix: add the exact source cap below, or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if summary.contains("no verified complete form for this read") => "review: no verified complete form for this read; expect `capped` and show what arrived",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if form && summary.contains("the closest form is") => "fix: use the closest form below (one arm stays `complete: false`), or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if form => "fix: use the complete form below, or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" => "fix: pin the scope, or accept `complete: false`",
        "N_DELETE_RESTRICTED" => "fix: declare the reference's `on delete`, or guard the delete",
        "N_DELETE_NEEDS_READ" => "review: some deleters get NOT_FOUND",
        "N_REFERENCE_UNCHECKED" => "review: this reference argument has no mandatory target lookup",
        "N_MAINTAIN_AUTHORIZATION" => "review: hidden and absent are different caller outcomes",
        "N_PATCH_CLEARS" => "review: an omitted argument clears this column",
        "N_WRITE_DENIED" if summary.contains("admits only 'service:") => "review: a seat require grants no table authority",
        "N_WRITE_DENIED" => "fix: this write refuses E_RULE for callers you admit",
        "N_REQUIRE_REFUSED" => "review: callers you admit may still get E_RULE",
        "N_QUERY_FILTERED" => "review: this query branch may filter rows under table authority",
        "N_READ_DENIED" => "fix: this read always returns null",
        "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" if summary.contains("get NOT_FOUND") => "review: the hidden lookup runs before this require",
        "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" => "fix: this require cannot see the rows it tests",
        "N_LOOKUP_NEVER_VISIBLE" => "fix: this nullable lookup is always null for the admitted callers",
        "N_LOOKUP_HIDDEN" => "review: a required read gate may hide rows from writers",
        "N_PROBE_ONLY_READ" | "N_PUBLIC_AGGREGATE_COPY" | "N_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_DISCLOSURE" => "review: who can read this",
        "N_VACUOUS_RULE" => "review: who can write this",
        "N_IMMUTABLE_PATCH" => "review: readable conflicts require the original immutable value",
        "N_NO_CURSOR" => "no action for a top-N list",
        _ => match tier(code) {
            Tier::Review => "review",
            Tier::Performance => "no action unless it is slow (`check --cost` measures it)",
            Tier::Design => "no action for an online app",
        },
    }
}

fn heading(tier: Tier) -> &'static str {
    match tier {
        Tier::Review => "review — can cost correctness or completeness:",
        Tier::Performance => "performance — costs speed, meter headroom or offline coverage:",
        Tier::Design => "by design — what a declaration implies; no action needed for online use:",
    }
}

pub(crate) struct Note {
    pub site: String,
    pub code: String,
    pub summary: String,
    /// An expanded compiled repair: its lead line and the code. Compact
    /// completeness notes leave this empty and keep the repair in `--verbose`.
    pub form: Option<(String, String)>,
    /// Three-line default rendering for completeness verdicts. Their full
    /// planner proof and compiled repair remain in `check --verbose`.
    pub compact: Option<[String; 3]>,
}

/// The compiled form leading a note's rewrite: the text before "Otherwise:",
/// when it holds code (a lead line ending in `:` and the declaration under it).
// @ref LLP 3000.036#15.5 — the compiled owner-or-X rewrite prints by default, once per form.
pub(crate) fn compiled_form(code: &str, rewrite: &str) -> Option<(String, String)> {
    if !matches!(code, "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" | "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE") { return None; }
    let head = rewrite.split("\n\nOtherwise:").next()?;
    let (lead, body) = head.split_once('\n')?;
    (lead.ends_with(':') && body.trim_start().starts_with("query ")).then(|| (lead.to_owned(), body.trim_end().to_owned()))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#2 — explicit saved bytes replace only source selection, never activation or store semantics.
use super::{Options, Project};
use std::path::Path;

/// Select one complete saved backend, or the ordinary multi-file project.
/// The vector is retained for both compilation and staging: one read, no swap.
pub(super) fn sources(project: &Project, options: &Options, flag: &str) -> Result<Vec<(String, String)>, String> {
    match options.flags.get(flag) {
        None => project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        Some(file) => {
            let path = Path::new(file);
            if !path.try_exists().map_err(|e|format!("cannot inspect saved source {file}: {e}"))? {return Err(format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved source does not exist"));}
            if path.is_dir() {
                return crate::client_compatibility::sources(path).map_err(|e| e.to_string());
            }
            let name = path.file_name().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).ok_or("saved source needs a UTF-8 file name")?;
            if !name.ends_with(".q") { return Err("saved source must be a complete Quarry .q file".into()); }
            let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|e| if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound {format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved source does not exist")} else {format!("cannot read saved source {file}: {e}")})?;
            if text.trim().is_empty() { return Err(format!("{file}: not saved yet: saved Quarry source is empty")); }
            // A saved complete backend retains the schema namespace regardless of its archive file name.
            Ok(vec![("schema.q".into(), text)])
        }
    }
}

pub(super) fn compile(sources: &[(String, String)], options: &Options, flag: &str) -> snapback4_lang::Bound {
    let mut bound = snapback4_lang::compile(sources);
    if let Some(file) = options.flags.get(flag) {
        for diagnostic in &mut bound.diagnostics { diagnostic.site.file = if Path::new(file).is_dir() { Path::new(file).join(&diagnostic.site.file).to_string_lossy().into_owned() } else { file.clone() }; }
    }
    bound
}

/// A saved file can be used outside a project. Companion code is selected
/// only from an actual current project, never from arbitrary sibling files.
pub(super) fn project(options: &Options, flag: &str) -> Result<Project, String> {
    let cwd = std::env::current_dir().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if let Some(project) = Project::locate(&cwd) { return Ok(project); }
    if options.flags.contains_key(flag) { return Ok(Project { root: cwd }); }
    Err("no snapback/*.q here or above; run `snapback4 init` or select a saved source file".into())
}

/// A private companion build uses the selected schema's generated contracts.
/// Packaging still records the original host installation, never this scratch.
pub(super) struct Candidate { pub project: Project, cleanup: std::path::PathBuf }
impl Drop for Candidate { fn drop(&mut self) { let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.cleanup); } }
impl Candidate {
    pub(super) fn prepare(original: &Project, stage: &Path, bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound) -> Result<Self, String> {
        use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering};
        static SERIAL: AtomicUsize = AtomicUsize::new(0);
        let root = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("snapback4-saved-source-{}-{}", std::process::id(), SERIAL.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)));
        std::fs::create_dir(&root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        #[cfg(unix)] snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let mut candidate = Self { project: Project { root: root.clone() }, cleanup: root };
        candidate.project.root = crate::dev_worker::private_scope(original, &candidate.cleanup)?;
        // The worker snapshotter captures the actual import closure and refuses
        // symlinks. Copy those captured bytes, not an unbounded application tree.
        copy_tree(stage, &candidate.project.root).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        // Discover the current dependency graph without installing selected
        // contracts in the working project. A failed typecheck still publishes
        // its graph; the copied bytes are checked with selected contracts below.
        if let Some(entry) = crate::dev_worker::source(original) {
            let _ = original.check_entry(&entry);
            crate::dev_worker::package_entry(original, &candidate.project.root)?;
        }
        candidate.project.write_generated_from(bound, original).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if !crate::native::declared(bound).is_empty() && original.native_manifest().exists() {
            copy_tree(&original.native_dir(), &candidate.project.native_dir()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            rebase_tree(&candidate.project.native_dir(), &original.native_dir()).map_err(|e| format!("saved-source native snapshot: {e}"))?;
        }
        crate::dev_worker::link_dependencies(original, &candidate.project.root)?;
        if let Some(entry) = crate::dev_worker::source(&candidate.project) { candidate.project.check_entry(&entry)?; }
        Ok(candidate)
    }
}
fn copy_tree(source: &Path, target: &Path) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    std::fs::create_dir_all(target)?;
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(source)? {
        let entry = entry?;
        if matches!(entry.file_name().to_str(), Some("target" | "native-target" | ".git")) { continue; }
        let kind = entry.file_type()?;
        if kind.is_dir() { copy_tree(&entry.path(), &target.join(entry.file_name()))?; }
        else if kind.is_file() { std::fs::copy(entry.path(), target.join(entry.file_name()))?; }
        else { return Err(std::io::Error::other("saved-source companion snapshot permits regular source files only")); }
    }
    Ok(())
}

    31	        let own=people.iter().filter(|(roster,_,_,_)|roster==table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    32	        if t.columns.values().filter(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)).count()>3 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: full Cartesian combinations above three principal columns remain uncovered"));}
    33	        let mut population=Vec::new();
    34	        for n in 0..counts[table]{
    35	            let person=if own.is_empty(){&people[n%people.len()]}else{own[n]};
    36	            let mut fields=BTreeMap::from([("id".into(),Value::Id(format!("security-{table}-{n}")))]);
    37	            let mut principal=0;
    38	            for (column,kind) in &t.columns {
    39	                if column=="id"||t.engine_field(column)||t.recorded.contains_key(column)||t.maintained.contains_key(column){continue;}
    40	                let mut value=if kind.is_optional()&&n%2==0&&!(person.0==*table&&person.1==*column){Value::Null}else{match sample(kind.required(),4+n){Some(value)=>value,None=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}.{column}: unsupported typed sample"));continue;}}};
    41	                if value==Value::Null&&kind.is_optional(){fields.insert(column.clone(),value);continue;}
    42	                match kind.required(){
    43	                    C::Principal=>{
    44	                        let linked=!own.is_empty()||grant_tables.contains(table)||t.constraints.iter().any(|constraint|matches!(constraint,Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{key,..} if key.iter().any(|term|matches!(term,RuleTerm::Field(c) if c==column))));
    45	                        value=Value::Id(if linked{if principal==0{person.3.clone()}else{people[(n+principal)%people.len()].3.clone()}}else{if (n>>principal.min(5))&1==0{VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into()});principal+=1;
    46	                    },
    47	                    C::Ref{table:target}=>{value=Value::Id(format!("security-{target}-{}",n%counts.get(target).copied().unwrap_or(1)));},
    48	                    C::Enum{..}|C::Text{..} if person.0==*table&&person.1==*column=>value=Value::String(person.2.clone()),
    49	                    C::Text{max}=>{let minimum=t.constraints.iter().filter_map(|c|if let Constraint::Text{field,min,..}=c{(field==column).then_some(*min as usize)}else{None}).max().unwrap_or(1);let suffix=format!("sample{n}");value=Value::String(format!("{suffix}{}","x".repeat(minimum.saturating_sub(suffix.len()))).chars().take(*max as usize).collect());},
    50	                    C::Time=>value=Value::Int(NOW+if t.expire.as_deref()==Some(column){86_400_000}else{-1000}),
    51	                    _=>{},
    52	                }
    53	                fields.insert(column.clone(),value);
    54	            }
    55	            population.push(Value::Row(fields.into()));
    56	        }
    57	        rows.insert(table.clone(),population);
    58	    }
    59	    // Batch validation preserves final-exists, parent ordering and maintains.
    60	    if let Ok(engine)=engine_with(bound,&rows,Vec::new()){return Ok(engine);}
    61	    if !out.reserve(1){return Err("generated fallback world not reserved; initialization uncovered".into());}
    62	    let mut engine=engine_with(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),Vec::new())?;
    63	    let mut pending=rows.into_iter().flat_map(|(table,rows)|rows.into_iter().map(move|row|(table.clone(),row))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    64	    let mut reasons=BTreeMap::new();
    65	    for _ in 0..=bound.schema.tables.len(){
    66	        let before=pending.len();let mut retry=Vec::new();
    67	        for (table,mut row) in pending {
    68	            if !out.reserve(1){validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;return Ok(engine);}
    69	            let attempt=(||{
    70	                for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[&table].columns {if let Ok(value)=row.field(column){let value=hydrate(&mut engine,kind,value)?;if let Value::Row(fields)=&mut row{fields.insert(column.clone(),value);}}}
    71	                engine.seed_at([(table.clone(),row.clone())],NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;Ok::<_,String>(())
    72	            })();
    73	            if let Err(why)=attempt{reasons.insert(table.clone(),why);retry.push((table,row));}
    74	        }
    75	        pending=retry;if pending.len()==before{break;}
    76	    }
    77	    for (table,why) in reasons{if pending.iter().any(|(name,_)|name==&table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: some related rows declined by ordinary validation: {why}"));}}
    78	    validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;
    79	    Ok(engine)
    80	}
    81	
    82	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#137-wave-19-correction-admissible-starts-and-persistent-refutations — creator guards/copies are part of generated initialization.
    83	pub fn fixture(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign)->Result<Engine,String>{
    84	    let proposed=proposals(bound,out)?;
    85	    let rows=readback(&proposed)?;
    86	    let actors=histories::actors(bound,&rows);
    87	    let rosters=bound.schema.visible_tables().filter(|(name,t)|t.columns.contains_key("role")||super::super::privilege::global_principal(bound,name).is_some()&&!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name).is_empty()).map(|(name,_)|name.clone()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    88	    let creators=bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native&&ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"insert"|"upsert"))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    89	    let created=creators.iter().flat_map(|p|ir::write_tables(&p.program)).filter(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"insert"|"upsert")).map(|(t,_)|t).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    90	    let mut engine=engine_on_store(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),Vec::new(),proposed.store().empty_with_asset_ledger().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?)?;
    91	    let mut pending=rows.iter().filter(|(t,_)|rosters.contains(*t)||!created.contains(*t)).flat_map(|(t,rs)|rs.iter().map(move|r|(t.clone(),r.clone()))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    92	    // Bootstrap proposals require an actor admitted by the insert rule in the
    93	    // previous world. Never let a proposed role authorize its own insertion.
    94	    for _ in 0..=bound.schema.tables.len(){
    95	        let before=pending.len();let mut retry=Vec::new();
    96	        for (table,row) in pending{
    97	            if !out.reserve(1){out.unsupported.insert("generated initialization work exhausted; remaining populations uncovered".into());validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;return Ok(engine);}
    98	            let gate=&bound.schema.tables[&table].rules.insert;
    99	            if !actors.iter().any(|actor|matches!(engine.fixture_policy(gate,actor,None,&row,NOW),Ok(rules::Verdict::Readable))) {retry.push((table,row));continue;}
   100	            let mut candidate=engine.fork_fixture().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   101	            let seeded=clean_row(bound,&table,&row);
   102	            if candidate.seed_at([(table.clone(),seeded)],NOW).is_ok(){engine=candidate;}else{retry.push((table,row));}
   103	        }
   104	        pending=retry;if pending.len()==before{break;}
   105	    }
   106	    for (table,_) in pending{out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: no admitted insert/bootstrap; population uncovered"));}
   107	    // Authored insertion bodies preserve requires, immutable copies, defaults,
   108	    // role checks on principal arguments and multi-table creation invariants.
   109	    let mut attempts=0usize;
   110	    for _ in 0..=bound.schema.tables.len().min(12){
   111	        let before=readback(&engine)?;
   112	        for bp in &creators{
   113	            let p=&bp.program;let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
   114	            if writes.iter().all(|(t,_)|rosters.contains(t)){continue;}
   115	            let current=readback(&engine)?;
   116	            let targets=writes.iter().filter(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"insert"|"upsert")).map(|(t,_)|t).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   117	            if targets.iter().all(|t|current.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)>=2){continue;}
   118	            let cases=histories::fixture_cases(p,&current,&actors,out);
   119	            'cases:for args in cases.into_iter().take(16){for actor in &actors{
   120	                if attempts>=2048||!out.reserve(1){out.unsupported.insert("generated authored initialization capped at 2048 calls; remaining creators uncovered".into());validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;return Ok(engine);}
   121	                attempts+=1;
   122	                let mut candidate=engine.fork_fixture().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   123	                let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut candidate,&p.args,&args,actor){Ok(a)=>a,Err(_)=>continue};
   124	                let result=candidate.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(actor,args).at(NOW));
   125	                if matches!(result,Ok(r) if matches!(r.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..})) {
   126	                    let after=readback(&candidate)?;
   127	                    if targets.iter().any(|t|after.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)>current.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)) {engine=candidate;}
   128	                    let after=readback(&engine)?;
   129	                    if targets.iter().all(|t|after.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)>=2){break 'cases;}
   130	                }
   131	            }}
   132	        }
   133	        if readback(&engine)?==before{break;}
   134	    }
   135	    for table in created.difference(&rosters){if engine.store().rows(table).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.is_empty(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: authored creator did not populate this table"));}}
   136	    validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;Ok(engine)
   137	}
   138	fn clean_row(bound:&Bound,table:&str,row:&Value)->Value{
   139	    let t=&bound.schema.tables[table];
   140	    match row{Value::Row(fields)=>Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect()),_=>row.clone()}
   141	}

     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — only actual fixture traces establish application reachability.
     3	use super::*;
     4	const LIMIT:usize=512;
     5	#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize)]
     6	pub struct Step {pub operation:String,pub viewer:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub result:serde_json::Value}
     7	
     8	pub(super) fn viewers(bound:&Bound)->Vec<String>{
     9	    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([VIEWER.into(),OTHER.into(),"account:security-no-role".into(),"service:security".into()]);
    10	    out.extend(bound.personas.iter().map(|p|if p.contains(':'){p.clone()}else{format!("dev:{p}")}));
    11	    fn seats(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match value{
    12	        serde_json::Value::String(s) if s.starts_with("service:")&&s.len()>8=>{out.insert(s.clone());},
    13	        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs{seats(x,out)},
    14	        serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for x in xs.values(){seats(x,out)},_=>{}
    15	    }}
    16	    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.schema).unwrap(),&mut out);
    17	    for p in &bound.programs{seats(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&mut out);}
    18	    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.invariants).unwrap(),&mut out);
    19	    out.into_iter().collect()
    20	}
    21	pub(super) fn actors(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>)->Vec<String>{
    22	    let mut out=viewers(bound);
    23	    for (table,rs) in rows{for row in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{
    24	        if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!out.contains(p){out.push(p.clone());}
    25	    }}}
    26	    out
    27	}
    28	pub(super) fn fixture_cases(p:&Program,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    29	    let args=&p.args;
    30	    let mut literals=BTreeMap::<String,Vec<Value>>::new();
    31	    fn collect(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>){
    32	        if let Some(binary)=value.get("Binary") {for (a,b) in [("left","right"),("right","left")] {
    33	            if let (Some(name),Some(literal))=(binary.get(a).and_then(|v|v.get("Variable")).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str),binary.get(b).and_then(|v|v.get("Literal"))) {
    34	                if let Ok(value)=serde_json::from_value::<Value>(literal.clone()){out.entry(name.into()).or_default().push(value);}
    35	            }
    36	        }}
    37	        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for v in xs.values(){collect(v,out)},serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for v in xs{collect(v,out)},_=>{}}
    38	    }
    39	    collect(&serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap(),&mut literals);
    40	    fn values(name:&str,k:&ir::ArgType,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String])->Option<Vec<Value>>{
    41	        Some(match k{
    42	            ir::ArgType::Enum{variants}=>variants.iter().map(|s|Value::String(s.clone())).collect(),
    43	            ir::ArgType::Text{min,max,..}=>['z','y'].into_iter().map(|c|Value::String(c.to_string().repeat((*min).max(1).min(*max) as usize))).collect(),
    44	            ir::ArgType::Int=>vec![Value::Int(0),Value::Int(1),Value::Int(2)],
    45	            ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max}=>{let mut xs=vec![*min,1i64.clamp(*min,*max),*max];xs.sort();xs.dedup();xs.into_iter().map(Value::Int).collect()},
    46	            ir::ArgType::Bool=>vec![Value::Bool(false),Value::Bool(true)],
    47	            ir::ArgType::Time=>vec![Value::Int(NOW-86_400_000),Value::Int(NOW),Value::Int(NOW+86_400_000)],
    48	            ir::ArgType::Principal=>actors.iter().map(|s|Value::Id(s.clone())).collect(),
    49	            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rows.get(table).is_some_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())=>rows[table].iter().filter_map(|r|r.field("id").ok().cloned()).collect(),
    50	            ir::ArgType::Optional(inner)=>{let mut v=vec![Value::Null];v.extend(values(name,inner,rows,actors)?);v},
    51	            _=>vec![arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.into(),k.clone())]),0)?.remove(name)?],
    52	        })
    53	    }
    54	    let mut cases=vec![BTreeMap::new()];
    55	    for (name,k) in args{
    56	        let Some(mut vals)=values(name,k,rows,actors)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{name}: history needs a typed asset argument"));return Vec::new();};
    57	        for literal in literals.get(name).into_iter().flatten() {
    58	            let valid=match (k,literal){
    59	                (ir::ArgType::Text{min,max,..},Value::String(s))=>s.chars().filter(|c|!c.is_whitespace()).count()>=*min as usize&&s.chars().count()<=*max as usize,
    60	                (ir::ArgType::Int,Value::Int(_))=>true,
    61	                (ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max},Value::Int(n))=>(*min..=*max).contains(n),_=>false,
    62	            };
    63	            if valid&&!vals.contains(literal){vals.insert(0,literal.clone());}
    64	        }
    65	        let mut next=Vec::new();
    66	        'product:for offset in 0..vals.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(vals.len()){
    67	            if out.exhausted(){return next;}
    68	            let mut case=cases[n%cases.len()].clone();case.insert(name.clone(),vals[(n+offset)%vals.len()].clone());
    69	            if !next.contains(&case){next.push(case);}if next.len()==LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert("history arguments capped at 512 combinations".into());break 'product;}
    70	        }}
    71	        cases=next;
    72	    }cases
    73	}
    74	pub(super) fn trace_key(steps:&[Step])->Vec<(&str,&str)>{steps.iter().map(|s|(s.operation.as_str(),s.viewer.as_str())).collect()}
    75	fn violation(out:&mut Campaign,inv:&Invariant,steps:&[Step],viewer:&str,row:&Value,old:Option<&Value>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,effect:&str)->bool{
    76	    if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){return false;}
    77	    out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace,observations:None,history:steps.to_vec(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:steps.last().map(|s|s.operation.clone()).unwrap_or("$fixture.read".into()),effect:effect.into(),row:row.clone(),old:old.cloned(),supporting_rows:initial.clone(),rule_path:"actual authored operation/read from the trusted fixture".into(),scope:"reachable from recorded trusted fixture; finite trace, not an all-history proof".into()});true
    78	}
    79	fn typed_disclosure(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value,column:Option<&str>,physical:&Value)->bool{
    80	    use crate::types::Ty;
    81	    let payload=|data:&Value|matches!(data,Value::Row(_))&&data.field("id").ok()==Some(key)&&column.is_none_or(|c|data.field(c).ok().is_some_and(|v|physical.field(c).ok()==Some(v)));
    82	    match ty{
    83	        Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t) if t==table=>payload(data),
    84	        Ty::Optional(inner)=>typed_disclosure(inner,data,table,key,column,physical),
    85	        Ty::Page(t,_) if t==table=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(payload)),
    86	        Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(|r|typed_disclosure(inner,r,table,key,column,physical))),
    87	        Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.iter().any(|(name,ty)|data.field(name).ok().is_some_and(|v|typed_disclosure(ty,v,table,key,column,physical))),
    88	        _=>false,
    89	    }
    90	}
    91	fn typed_source(ty:&crate::types::Ty,table:&str)->bool{
    92	    use crate::types::Ty;
    93	    match ty{Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t)|Ty::Page(t,_)=>t==table,Ty::Optional(inner)|Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>typed_source(inner,table),Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.values().any(|ty|typed_source(ty,table)),_=>false}
    94	}
    95	pub(super) fn observer_tables(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
    96	    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([table.into()]);
    97	    loop{let before=out.len();
    98	        for m in &bound.schema.maintains{if out.contains(&m.source){out.insert(m.target.clone());if let Some(owner)=&m.private{out.insert(owner.table.clone());}}}
    99	        for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables{if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|edge|out.contains(&edge.parent))||[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|t|out.contains(t))){out.insert(name.clone());}}
   100	        if out.len()==before{return out;}
   101	    }
   102	}
   103	fn reserve_observers(out:&mut Campaign,calls:usize)->bool{
   104	    if !out.reserve(calls){return false;}
   105	    if out.history_observer_calls+calls>32768{out.unsupported.insert("history read observers capped at 32768 calls; remaining disclosure combinations not covered".into());false}else{out.history_observer_calls+=calls;true}
   106	}
   107	fn observe(bound:&Bound,e:&mut Engine,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
   108	    if !reserve_observers(out,0)||out.history_observer_calls>=32768{return;}
   109	    let queries=bound.programs.iter().filter(|bp|bp.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!bp.program.native).filter(|bp|{
   110	        let json=serde_json::to_string(&bp.program).unwrap();
   111	        if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|tag|json.contains(tag)){
   112	            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque history observer needs disclosure provenance",bp.program.name));false
   113	        }else{true}
   114	    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   115	    if queries.is_empty(){return;}
   116	    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"){
   117	        let direct=inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],inv.column.as_deref()),p)==Ok(true));
   118	        let indirect=bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)));
   119	        if direct&&!indirect{continue;}
   120	
   121	        let observing=observer_tables(bound,&inv.table);
   122	        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){'actors:for viewer in actors{
   123	            if out.exhausted(){return;}
   124	            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){continue;}
   125	            if !reserve_observers(out,1){return;}
   126	            match policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,None,row,e){Ok(false)=>{},Ok(true)=>continue,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {why}",inv.target_label()));continue;}}
   127	            let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
   128	            if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].written_by.get(c)).is_some_and(|gate|!gate.audience&&row.field(&gate.owner).ok()!=Some(&Value::Id(viewer.clone()))){continue 'actors;}
   129	
   130	            // A typed authored row return is already a payload witness. It does
   131	            // not require an invalid absent world that deletes referenced data.
   132	            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|typed_source(&p.result,&inv.table)){
   133	                let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program,rows,actors,out);
   134	                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read-observer arguments capped at 32",bp.program.name));}
   135	                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
   136	                    if !reserve_observers(out,2){return;}
