#[test]
fn hidden_reference_hops_stay_unknown_and_expiry_is_not_an_authored_delete(){
 let source="table parents:\n  owner: principal\n  active: bool\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\ntable notes:\n  parent: parents\n  until: time\n  read <- allow\n  update <- allow\n  expire at .until\n  online only\ninvariant notes.read only .parent.active != true\ninvariant notes.delete only deny\nquery inspect(note: notes):\n  return notes[note]\nmutation expire(note: notes):\n  update notes[note] { until: now }\n  return null\n";
 let b=compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source.into())]);assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);
 let now=1_800_000_000_000;let mut fixture=Engine::new_at(snapback4_core::Store::memory().unwrap(),b.schema.clone(),b.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone()),now).unwrap();
 fixture.seed_at([("parents".into(),row(&[("id",Value::Id("p".into())),("owner",Value::Id("dev:fixture-owner".into())),("active",Value::Bool(true))])),("notes".into(),row(&[("id",Value::Id("n".into())),("parent",Value::Id("p".into())),("until",Value::Int(now+100_000))]))],now).unwrap();
 let mut c=security::challenges::Campaign::default();security::challenges::operations(&b,&fixture,&mut c);
 assert!(c.violations.iter().all(|v|v.effect=="read"&&v.viewer=="dev:fixture-owner"),"{c:?}");assert!(c.unsupported.iter().any(|s|s.contains("unknown")));assert!(c.unsupported.iter().any(|s|s.contains("not an executed delete")));
pub fn apply(report:&mut Report,campaign:&Campaign){
    report.campaign_coverage.extend(campaign.unsupported.iter().cloned());
    let rank=|c:&Counterexample|match c.reachability{Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace=>0,Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace=>1,_=>2};
    let committed=|c:&Counterexample|c.history.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count();
    for c in &campaign.violations {for v in &mut report.invariants {if v.invariant.site.to_string()!=c.invariant_site{continue;}
        if v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|old|rank(old)<rank(c)||rank(old)==rank(c)&&committed(old)<=committed(c)){continue;}
        v.status=Status::Refuted;v.counterexample=Some(c.clone());
        v.reasons.push("concrete policy-false snapshot admitted by the actual engine; fixture origin is explicit, not an empty-state reachability theorem".into());
    }}
}

fn arguments(args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,variant:usize)->Option<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    use ir::ArgType as A;
    fn one(k:&A,v:usize)->Option<Value>{Some(match k{
        A::Ref{table}=>id(table),A::Principal=>Value::Id(if v&1==0{OTHER}else{VIEWER}.into()),A::Id=>Value::Id("security-argument".into()),A::Cursor|A::Optional(_)=>Value::Null,
//! Advice identifies a risk shape; it never supplies a proof assumption.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#123-actionable-authority-advice — current roles, grant writers, required evidence and refusal oracles.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Expr,Stmt,ProgramKind};
const NOW_FOR_DEFAULTS:i64=1_800_000_000_000;
fn rule_parts(rule:&RuleExpr)->Vec<&RuleExpr>{match rule{RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().flat_map(rule_parts).collect(),RuleExpr::Not(x)=>rule_parts(x),r=>vec![r]}}
fn role_test(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr)->bool {rule_parts(rule).iter().any(|r|match r{
    RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..} if key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>{
        if super::privilege::membership_probe(bound,r,table){return true;}
        let columns=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
        super::privilege::probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities).iter().any(|(c,t)|columns.contains(c)&&matches!(t,RuleTerm::Literal(_)))
    },_=>false,
})}
fn reader_columns(rule:&RuleExpr)->BTreeSet<String>{rule_parts(rule).into_iter().filter_map(|r|match r{RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Viewer)|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(f))=>Some(f.clone()),_=>None}).collect()}
fn note(notes:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>,site:&Site,code:&str,message:String,rewrite:&str){notes.push(Diagnostic::new(site,code,message,rewrite).guide("security"));}
fn mutable_role(bound:&Bound,name:&str)->bool {
    let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(name)else{return false;};
    let mut roles=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name);
    if roles.is_empty()&&let Some(principal)=super::privilege::global_principal(bound,name){roles.insert(principal);}
    if roles.is_empty(){return false;}
    let mutable=roles.iter().any(|c|!t.constraints.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(c))));
    fn supplied(value:&serde_json::Value,roles:&BTreeSet<String>)->bool{
        if let Some(fields)=value.get("Object").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object){return fields.keys().any(|c|roles.contains(c));}
        if let Some(fields)=value.get("Literal").and_then(|v|v.get("Map").or_else(||v.get("Row"))).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object){return fields.keys().any(|c|roles.contains(c));}
        true // Dynamic patches cannot establish that role fields are untouched.
    }
    fn writes(value:&serde_json::Value,name:&str,roles:&BTreeSet<String>,update:bool,delete:bool)->bool{
        match value{
            serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>{
                for tag in ["Update","Upsert","WriteTarget"]{if let Some(body)=xs.get(tag){
                    let target=if tag=="WriteTarget"{body.get("target").and_then(|v|v.get("table"))}else{body.get("table")};
                    if update&&target.and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(name)&&body.get(if tag=="Upsert"{"row"}else{"patch"}).is_some_and(|v|supplied(v,roles)){return true;}
                }}
                for tag in ["Delete","DeleteTarget"]{if let Some(body)=xs.get(tag){
                    let target=if tag=="DeleteTarget"{body.get("target").and_then(|v|v.get("table"))}else{body.get("table")};
                    if delete&&target.and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(name){return true;}
                }}
                xs.values().any(|v|writes(v,name,roles,update,delete))
            },serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|writes(v,name,roles,update,delete)),_=>false,
        }
    }
    bound.programs.iter().any(|p|{
        let system=p.program.kind==snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job;
        let update=mutable&&(system||!matches!(t.rules.update,RuleExpr::Deny)||roles.iter().any(|c|t.written_by.contains_key(c)));
        let delete=system||!matches!(t.rules.delete,RuleExpr::Deny);
        writes(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),name,&roles,update,delete)
    })
}
fn recipient_role(bound:&Bound,name:&str,branch:&RuleExpr,grant:Option<&str>,roster:&str,effect:&str)->bool{
    if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==*name&&(i.target==effect||i.target=="write"&&matches!(effect,"insert"|"update"))&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::dependencies(p).contains(roster)&&role_test(bound,p)&&kernel::implies(branch,p)!=Ok(true))){return true;}
    // This is conditional lifetime advice, not an inferred current-role
    // contract: the retained grant does not itself consult this mutable roster.
    grant.is_some()&&!kernel::dependencies(branch).contains(roster)
}
pub(super) fn notes(bound:&Bound,exposures:&[Exposure])->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    let mut notes=Vec::new();
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#131-intent-and-diagnostics — equivalence is advice, never refusal.
    for inv in &bound.invariants {
        if inv.operation.is_some()||inv.target=="always" {continue;}
        let Some(policy)=&inv.policy else{continue;};
        let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
        let restates=super::effects(inv).iter().all(|word|{
            if matches!(*word,"insert"|"update")&&!table.written_by.is_empty(){return false;}
            let gate=if *word=="read"{super::columns::admission(table,inv.column.as_deref())}else if *word=="update"{super::images::stable(table,&table.update_rule_judged())}else{table.rules.for_effect(super::effect(word)).clone()};
            let intent=if *word=="update"{super::images::stable(table,&policy.old_and_next())}else{policy.clone()};
            kernel::implies(&gate,&intent)==Ok(true)&&kernel::implies(&intent,&gate)==Ok(true)
        });
        if restates {note(&mut notes,&inv.site,"N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE",format!("{}: this invariant restates the rule and can't catch a mistake in it; state the spec's intent instead",inv.target_label()),"rewrite from the spec: who may see/do what, for which resource and lifecycle state? Declare sensitive-column intent and current-role/relationship conditions independently; review security-invariants.q.draft");}
    }
    for (name,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
        if t.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable(){note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_BROAD_READ",format!("{name} is readable by every signed-in user: {}",t.rules.read.quarry()),"confirm the spec permits these columns always, or only while a lifecycle condition holds, and declare read intent; snapback4 audit lists inherited grants and their writers");}
        if let RuleExpr::Exists{table:roster,key,equalities,..}=&t.rules.read
            && equalities.is_empty()&&key.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer)) {
            note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_BROAD_READ",format!("{name} is readable by any signed-in user with a matching {roster} row; no particular role or row ownership is required"),"confirm that every roster member should see all these columns; declare intent from the privacy spec and inspect audit");
        }
        // A role-wide board branch returns the entire row, including private payload.
        if t.columns.values().any(|kind|kind.contains_asset())||bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==*name&&i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_some()) {
            for branch in t.rules.read.disjuncts() {
                let parts=rule_parts(branch);
                let role=parts.iter().any(|part|role_test(bound,part));
                let row_filter=parts.iter().any(|part|matches!(part,RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(_),RuleTerm::Literal(_))|RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Field(_),RuleTerm::Literal(_))|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(_),RuleTerm::Field(_))|RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Literal(_),RuleTerm::Field(_))));
                let unscoped=parts.iter().all(|part|match part{
                    RuleExpr::Exists{key,equalities,..}=>key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
                    _=>!part.has_reference(),
                })&&reader_columns(branch).is_empty();
                if role&&row_filter&&unscoped {note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_ROLE_WIDE_READ",format!("{name}.read: role-wide branch `{}` returns private or media columns for every matching row",branch.quarry()),"publish a board/projection table containing only intended public fields; protect private columns with independent column-read intent");break;}
            }
        }
        for (column,kind) in &t.columns {
            use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType as C;
            if kind.contains_asset() && (kind.is_optional() || matches!(kind.required(),C::List{..}|C::ListRange{min:0,..})) {
                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE",format!("{name}.{column} permits omitted or empty media evidence"),"if the spec requires evidence, declare a required image/file or [image] 1..N; otherwise retain optionality deliberately (guide security)");
            }
        }
        for leaf in rule_parts(&t.rules.insert) {
            if let RuleExpr::Created {table:parent,..}=leaf
                && bound.schema.table(parent).is_some_and(|p| p.rules.insert.disjuncts().iter().any(|r| matches!(r,RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(_),RuleTerm::Viewer)|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(_)))))
                && bound.schema.tables.values().any(|other| [&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete].iter().any(|r| rule_parts(r).iter().any(|r|matches!(r,RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,equalities,..} if table==name&&matches!(key.as_slice(),[RuleTerm::Viewer])&&equalities.is_empty())))) {
                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_VACUOUS_RULE",format!("{name}: `created {parent}` can bootstrap authority trusted globally by a viewer-only probe; a caller may create their own global administrator grant"),"provision global authority only through an operator/service seat or existing authorized admin; group creation must scope every later grant to that group (guide deploy, first-admin setup)");
            }

## 8. Operation and general row assertions (SEC refinement)

The table effect contract is intentionally stronger than a query/mutation
contract. To state an operation-specific authority rule without overrestricting
other writers, `invariant vehicles.liftHold only exists roster[viewer, role =
'liaison']` targets the unique authored mutation `liftHold` on vehicles.
Qualified operation names are allowed; ambiguous/unknown names are errors.
Only writes to the named table by that operation are covered. Its earlier
straight-line requirements may establish admission when they bind to existing
rule predicates: positive caller-filtered existence implies raw existence,
whereas negative existence is not assumed equivalent. Branch/loop guards,
aliases, helper calls, changed viewer, prior writes, and unsupported flow remain
unproved unless separately reduced. The current conservative implementation
uses only leading straight-line requirements before any other statement, and
only requirements without negative probes or live references. These require
facts are evaluated before writes; a policy depending on a table any earlier
statement can change cannot be proved. Require-to-write stability is checked
against every intervening write. Failure to establish it is unproved. The
actual effective write rule remains an additional obligation, including the
field-owner bypass and implicit effects. No operation syntax grants authority.

General `always` expressions can still receive concrete counterexamples even
when outside the proof fragment. Preserve the original policy AST; a separate
partial concrete evaluator can judge row-local values, Boolean connectives,
comparisons, server clock and raw named-key existence over a schema-valid
snapshot. Unsupported/unknown evaluation is not false and cannot refute.
Nonunique raw existence is an explicit existential over all stored live rows,
not a fabricated unique index or a caller-filtered program read. Unmodeled
expiry/captures/derived tables block this evaluator. Snapshot-seeded violations
are labeled as such, never reachable application traces. Actual authored
operation challenges use the engine, check committed changed images and name
the operation and arguments; no-op/rolled-back or merely successful unrelated
operations cannot refute a write policy. Fixtures and generated cross-principal
role rows may supply candidates, but never proof assumptions. Multi-operation
history, HTTP and device traces remain separately named coverage obligations.

### 8.1 Exact conservative boundary (review clarification)

//! Narrow stable leading-require reduction for operation-specific authority.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#81-exact-conservative-boundary-review-clarification — unknown write sets never establish stability.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Stmt,ProgramDecl,ProgramKind,InvariantDecl,RuleDecl};
use super::super::catalog::TableInfo;
pub(super) fn resolve<'a>(catalog:&'a Catalog,decl:&InvariantDecl)->Option<(String,&'a ProgramDecl)>{
    if matches!(decl.target.as_str(),"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"always"|"write"){return None;}
    let matches=catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|{let module=std::path::Path::new(&d.file).file_stem().unwrap().to_string_lossy();d.programs.iter().map(move|p|(super::super::operation_name(&module,&p.name),p))})
        .filter(|(name,p)|p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&(name==&decl.target||p.name==decl.target)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if matches.len()==1 {matches.into_iter().next()}else{None}
}
pub(super) fn safe(rule:&RuleExpr)->bool {match rule{
    RuleExpr::Exists{key,equalities,..}=>key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
    RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().all(safe),
    RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)=>[a,b].iter().all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
    RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Deny|RuleExpr::Public{..}=>true,_=>false,
}}
pub(super) fn requirements(catalog:&Catalog,info:&TableInfo,p:&ProgramDecl)->Vec<RuleExpr>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for s in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body){let Stmt::Require{condition,site,..}=s else{break};
        let decl=RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Read,bindings:Vec::new(),value:condition.clone(),site:site.clone()};
        if let Ok(rule)=super::super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&decl)&&safe(&rule){out.push(rule);}
    }out
}
pub(super) fn prove(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Result<bool,String>{
    let p=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|Some(&p.program.name)==inv.operation.as_ref()).ok_or("operation did not bind")?;
    if !safe(inv.policy.as_ref().ok_or("unsupported policy")?){return Err("operation policy needs unsupported target-image, negative-probe or live-reference bindings".into());}
    let writes=snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&p.program);
    if !writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table){return Err("operation has no direct write to the named table; implicit effects require a reduction".into());}
    let dependencies=inv.requirements.iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    if writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|dependencies.contains(t)) {return Err("require dependency can be changed by this operation, including its target write".into());}
    if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.maintained.is_empty()||!t.sequences.is_empty()||t.on_delete.values().any(|a|a.writes())) || !bound.schema.maintains.is_empty(){return Err("implicit maintain/sequence/referential writes require dependency stability reduction".into());}
    // Source helpers/control flow may hide write sets or viewer changes.
    let json=serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap();
    fn opaque(v:&serde_json::Value)->bool{match v{serde_json::Value::Object(m)=>m.keys().any(|k|matches!(k.as_str(),"For"|"If"|"JobAs"|"JobQuery"|"Emit"))||m.values().any(opaque),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(opaque),_=>false}}
    if opaque(&json){return Err("operation control flow or opaque effects are outside stable leading-require reduction".into());}
    let admission=RuleExpr::And(inv.requirements.clone());
    // Table-rule gates can only further restrict a dominating stable require;
    // using the require alone overapproximates effective owner-field gates.
    kernel::implies(&admission,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())
}

    Const { name: String, value: Expr },
    Let { name: String, value: Expr },

    Require {
        condition: Expr,
        code: String,
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
        site: Option<String>,
        /// The author's text for the refusal (`require … else CODE 'message'`); the code stays the contract.
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
        message: Option<String>,
    },

    Return(Expr),
    Break,
    Continue,
}

binary='/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b18-sec4/snapback4/target/debug/snapback4'
source='''table homes:
  owner: principal
  read <- allow
  insert <- allow
  online only
table reports:
  home: homes
  homeowner: principal
  immutable home,homeowner
  read <- .homeowner = viewer
  insert <- allow
  online only
mutation file(home: homes, homeowner: principal):
  payload = {home,homeowner}
  h = homes[home] "'! else MISSING
  require payload.homeowner = h.owner else WRONG_OWNER
  return insert reports payload
query mine():
  return reports where .homeowner = viewer first 50 by .id
invariant reports.homeowner read only .home.owner = viewer
'"'''
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='sec4-independent-guard-') as d:
 p=pathlib.Path(d);(p/'snapback').mkdir();(p/'fixtures').mkdir();(p/'snapback/schema.q').write_text(source)

//! Observer proofs and executed counterexamples, including negative controls.
use snapback4_lang::{compile,security::{self,Status}};
fn bound(source:&str)->snapback4_lang::Bound {
    let b=compile(&[("policy.q".into(),source.into())]);
    assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);b
}
#[test]
fn live_parent_reads_do_not_discharge_the_whole_invariant() {
    for policy in ["allow", ".project.owner = viewer"] {
        let b = bound(&format!("{}\ninvariant tasks.read only {policy}\n", include_str!("fixtures/live-parent.q")));
        let report = security::analyze(&b);
        let verdict = &report.invariants[0];
        assert_eq!(verdict.status, Status::Unproved, "{report:?}");
        assert!(verdict.reasons.iter().any(|r| r.contains("live-parent")), "{report:?}");
        if policy != "allow" {
            assert_eq!(verdict.direct_rule, Status::Unproved);
            assert!(verdict.counterexample.is_none());
        }
    }
}
fn aggregate(rule:&str,kind:&str)->String {let kind=if kind=="sum"{"sum notes of .amount"}else{"count notes"};format!("table notes:\n  owner: principal\n  amount: int\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\nview totals: {kind} by .owner\n  read <- {rule}\n  online only\nquery total(owner: principal):\n  return totals[owner]\ninvariant notes.read only .owner = viewer\n")}
#[test]
fn maintained_count_and_sum_preserve_or_expose_source_privacy(){
    for kind in ["count","sum"] {
        let b=bound(&aggregate(".owner = viewer",kind));
        let r=security::analyze(&b);
        assert!(r.invariants[0].obligations.iter().all(|o|o.status==Status::Proved),"{r:?}");
        assert!(security::challenges::run(&b).violations.is_empty());
        let b=bound(&aggregate("allow",kind));
        let r=security::analyze(&b);
        assert!(r.invariants[0].obligations.iter().any(|o|o.status==Status::Unproved));
        let c=security::challenges::run(&b);
        assert!(c.violations.iter().any(|v|v.observations.is_some()),"{c:?}");
    }
}
#[test]
fn lifetime_components_prove_exact_boundaries_with_nullable_edges(){
    for optional in ["", " ?"] {
        let lower=if optional.is_empty(){"now >= .opensAt"}else{".opensAt = null or now >= .opensAt"};
        let upper=if optional.is_empty(){"now < .endsAt"}else{".endsAt = null or now < .endsAt"};
        let b=bound(&format!("table bookings:\n  opensAt: time{optional}\n  endsAt: time{optional}\n  not before .opensAt\n  expire at .endsAt\n  read <- allow\n  online only\ninvariant bookings.read only ({lower}) and ({upper})\n"));
        let r=security::analyze(&b);
        assert_eq!(r.invariants[0].direct_rule,Status::Proved,"{r:?}");
        assert!(r.invariants[0].obligations.iter().all(|o|o.status==Status::Proved));
        // Global activity remains separately unproved; this is a component law.
        assert_eq!(r.invariants[0].status,Status::Unproved);
        let late=bound("table bookings:\n  opensAt: time\n  read <- allow\n  online only\ninvariant bookings.read only now >= .opensAt\n");
        assert_eq!(security::analyze(&late).invariants[0].direct_rule,Status::Unproved);
        assert!(!security::challenges::run(&late).violations.is_empty(),"an actual pre-publication read refutes the missing lifetime gate");
    }
}
#[test]
fn project_note_draft_and_uncovered_effects_are_independent(){
    let b=bound("table notes:\n  owner: principal\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\ntable publicNotes:\n  public 'published notes'\n  online only\n");
    let r=security::analyze(&b);
    assert_eq!(r.notes.iter().filter(|n|n.code=="N_SECURITY_INTENT").count(),1);
    assert!(r.notes.iter().any(|n|n.message.contains("2 tables have access rules")));
    let draft=security::draft(&b);assert!(draft.contains("DRAFT: NOT ACCEPTED"));
    assert!(!draft.contains("invariant notes.read only .owner = viewer"));
    assert!(draft.contains("notes.owner")&&draft.contains("APP SPECIFICATION"));
    let b=bound(&format!("{}\ninvariant notes.read only .owner = viewer\n", "table notes:\n  owner: principal\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\n"));
    let r=security::analyze(&b);
    assert!(r.uncovered.iter().any(|e|e.table=="notes"&&e.effect=="insert"));
    assert!(!r.uncovered.iter().any(|e|e.table=="notes"&&e.effect=="read"));
    assert!(r.text().ends_with("notes.insert: caller satisfying .owner = viewer; rows: .owner = viewer\n"));
}

codex
I reproduced a false positive outside the supplied controls: a creator inserts a report, then checks the inserted row’s homeowner against the home’s owner before committing. The coupling check counts direct argument uses and misses that check through the inserted-row alias. `check` then changes only the report’s homeowner in its alternate world and reports a failing authored-fixture disclosure from a consistent fixture.

//! `test.cases.jsonl` beside the fixture tables: the application's own
//! operations, in order, as named viewers, each with the outcome it expects.
//! One line is `{"as": "alice", "op": "addCard", "args": {..}, "expect": "sent"}`;
//! `expect` is `sent` (a mutation's default), `complete`, `capped`, `ok` (any
//! answer), or a refusal code (`E_RULE`, `NOT_FOUND`, a program's own `require`
//! code). A query's default is its `check` verdict: `complete`, or either
//! answer when `check` notes the query is never complete (N_NEVER_COMPLETE).
//! Optional `data` must equal the answer; `count` is the answer's length; `save`
//! names the answer so a later string argument `"$name"` or `"$name.field"`
//! reuses it. `as` is a declared persona, as on dev: an undeclared one is
//! refused E_AUTH.
use crate::project::persona_principal;
use snapback4_core::engine::refusal_trace::RefusalTrace;
use snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind;
use snapback4_core::{Context, Engine, Refusal, WriteState, wire};
use snapback4_lang::Bound;
use serde_json::{Value as Json, json};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::path::Path;

pub const FILE: &str = "test.cases.jsonl";

/// Dev's cast check, for `test` and a local `run`: with `use personas`
/// declared, a bare name (or `dev:<name>`) outside the cast is refused as dev
/// refuses its header (E_AUTH); a principal id such as `user:…` passes as itself.
pub fn undeclared_persona(name: &str, declared: &[String]) -> Option<String> {
    if declared.is_empty() { return None; }
    let bare = match name.split_once(':') { None => name, Some(("dev", bare)) => bare, Some(_) => return None };
    (!declared.iter().any(|p| p == bare)).then(|| format!(
        "persona {bare} is not declared; `use personas {}` is this project's cast, and dev refuses {bare} with E_AUTH (401). Add {bare} to it, or use a declared persona (an outsider is a declared persona with no rows)",
        declared.join(", ")))
}

/// The persona a case without `as` runs as: alice when she is in the cast (or
/// there is none), else the first declared persona.
pub fn default_persona(declared: &[String]) -> &str {
    if declared.is_empty() || declared.iter().any(|p| p == "alice") { "alice" } else { &declared[0] }
}

/// One case as `test`'s query sweep reads it: which query, as whom, with
/// which arguments (`None` when they name a saved answer, which only the case
/// run has), expecting what.
pub struct Explicit { pub at: String, pub op: String, pub principal: String, pub args: Option<Json>, pub expect: Option<String> }

/// The cases file read leniently, before the sweep: a line that does not
/// parse is left to [`run`], which refuses it with its line number.
// @ref LLP 3000.036#15.2 — wave 7: the sweep runs a persona's own case arguments and labels a refusal its case expects.
pub fn explicit(fixture: &str, bound: &Bound, now: i64) -> Vec<Explicit> {
    let declared = &bound.personas;
    let Ok(text) = std::fs::read_to_string(Path::new(fixture).join(FILE)) else { return Vec::new() };
    fn saved(value: &Json) -> bool {
        match value { Json::String(text) => text.starts_with('$') && text.len() > 1, Json::Array(items) => items.iter().any(saved), Json::Object(fields) => fields.len() == 1 && fields.contains_key("$literal") || fields.values().any(saved), _ => false }
    }
    text.lines().enumerate().filter_map(|(index, line)| {
        let line = line.trim();
        if line.is_empty() || line.starts_with("//") { return None; }
        let case: Json = serde_json::from_str(line).ok()?;
        let op = case.get("op")?.as_str()?.to_owned();
        let persona = case.get("as").and_then(Json::as_str).unwrap_or_else(|| default_persona(declared));
        let principal = if persona.contains(':') { persona.to_owned() } else { persona_principal(persona) };
        let mut args = Some(case.get("args").cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| json!({}))).filter(|args| args.is_object() && !saved(args));
        if let Some(args) = &mut args && let Some(program) = bound.programs.iter().find(|program| program.program.name == op) {
            super::test_fixture::argument_times(&program.program.args, args, now).ok()?;
        }
        Some(Explicit { at: format!("{FILE}:{}", index + 1), op, principal, args, expect: case.get("expect").and_then(Json::as_str).map(str::to_owned) })
    }).collect()
}

/// Runs every case; prints one line each and returns (passed, failed).
/// `sources` are the compiled files, to quote a refusing clause's line.
pub fn run(engine: &mut Engine, bound: &Bound, fixture: &str, sources: &[(String, String)], now: i64) -> Result<(usize, usize), String> {
    run_verbose(engine, bound, fixture, sources, now, false)
}

pub(super) fn run_verbose(engine: &mut Engine, bound: &Bound, fixture: &str, sources: &[(String, String)], now: i64, verbose: bool) -> Result<(usize, usize), String> {
    run_presented(engine, bound, fixture, sources, now, false, verbose, |_, _| {})
}

pub(super) fn run_observed(engine: &mut Engine, bound: &Bound, fixture: &str, sources: &[(String, String)], now: i64, quiet: bool, observed: impl FnMut(&str, &str)) -> Result<(usize, usize), String> {
    run_presented(engine, bound, fixture, sources, now, quiet, false, observed)
}

fn run_presented(engine: &mut Engine, bound: &Bound, fixture: &str, sources: &[(String, String)], now: i64, quiet: bool, verbose: bool, mut observed: impl FnMut(&str, &str)) -> Result<(usize, usize), String> {
    macro_rules! case_line { ($($args:tt)*) => { if !quiet { println!($($args)*); } }; }
    let path = Path::new(fixture).join(FILE);
    let text = match std::fs::read_to_string(&path) {
        Ok(text) => text,
        Err(error) if error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok((0, 0)),
        Err(error) => return Err(format!("cannot read {}: {error}", path.display())),
    };
    let mut saved = BTreeMap::<String, Json>::new();
    let (mut passed, mut failed) = (0, 0);
    let mut verdicts_shown = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
    let lines: Vec<(usize, &str)> = text.lines().enumerate().filter(|(_, line)| !line.trim().is_empty() && !line.trim_start().starts_with("//")).collect();
    // Every line is checked before any runs: a misspelled key must not pass
    // silently (wave 5: `"rows":0` passed against one row), and a typo on the
    // last line should not wait for every earlier case.
    let mut cases = Vec::with_capacity(lines.len());
    for (index, line) in &lines {
        let at = format!("{FILE}:{}", index + 1);

    32	fn path(project:&Project)->std::path::PathBuf{project.root.join("snapback/generated/security-test.json")}
    33	pub(in crate::verbs) fn remember(project:&Project,options:&Options,campaign:&security::challenges::Campaign,complete:bool,consumed:&str)->Result<(),String>{
    34	    if fingerprint(project,options)?!=consumed{return Err("source or fixtures changed during test; evidence was not saved; rerun snapback4 test".into());}
    35	    let mut record=Record{fingerprint:consumed.into(),complete,attempted:campaign.attempted,sequences:campaign.sequences,coverage_gaps:campaign.unsupported.len(),violations:campaign.violations.iter().filter(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable()).cloned().collect()};
    36	    let prior=matching(project,options).map(|r|r.violations).unwrap_or_default();
    37	    let mut seen=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
    38	    record.violations=prior.into_iter().chain(record.violations).filter(|v|v.reachability.is_reachable()&&seen.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(&v.invariant_site,&v.viewer,&v.operation,&v.effect,&v.reachability)).unwrap())).collect();
    39	    let path=path(project);std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    40	    let temp=path.with_extension(format!("{}.tmp",std::process::id()));
    41	    let result=std::fs::write(&temp,serde_json::to_vec(&record).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?).and_then(|_|std::fs::rename(&temp,&path));
    42	    if result.is_err(){let _=std::fs::remove_file(temp);}result.map_err(|e|e.to_string())
    43	}
    44	pub(super) fn matching(project:&Project,options:&Options)->Option<Record>{
    45	    let path=path(project);
    46	    let record:Record=serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(path).ok()?).ok()?;
    47	    (record.fingerprint==fingerprint(project,options).ok()?).then_some(record)
    48	}

**NOT READY** for source commit `afcff3d2d7`: one reproduced material false positive remains.

[ column_reads.rs:77 ](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b18-sec4/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/security/challenges/column_reads.rs:77) treats an argument used once as independently perturbable. It misses creator guards that inspect that value through the inserted-row alias. This violates [section 13.7’s coupling requirement](/home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b18-sec4/llp/3000.036.004-security-invariants-and-exposure.rfc.md:1386).

Reproducer schema:

```quarry
table homes:
  owner: principal
  read <- allow
  insert <- allow
  online only
table reports:
  home: homes
  homeowner: principal
  immutable home,homeowner
  read <- .homeowner = viewer
  insert <- allow
  online only
mutation file(home: homes, homeowner: principal):
  r = insert reports {home,homeowner}
  h = homes[home] ! else MISSING
  require r.homeowner = h.owner else WRONG_OWNER
  return r
query mine():
  return reports where .homeowner = viewer first 50 by .id
invariant reports.homeowner read only .home.owner = viewer
```

Fixtures:

- `homes.jsonl`: `{"id":"h","owner":"dev:owner"}`
- `reports.jsonl`: `{"id":"r","home":"h","homeowner":"dev:owner"}`

Observed results:

- `check --json` exits **1**, attributing an `authored-fixture-trace` disclosure to `mine` for `dev:security-a`.
- Its actual observation is `[]`. The alternate world changes only `reports.homeowner` to that viewer, breaking the creator’s enforced relationship, then returns the report.
- Ordinary execution correctly rejects the mismatched creator call with `WRONG_OWNER`, leaves the outsider’s query empty, and accepts the matching owner’s call. All four business cases passed.
- `test --fixture-only` nevertheless reports **31 reachable violations**, persists one with `complete: true`, and subsequent `check` reuses the false refutation.

The reduction must conservatively decline this guarded field unless it establishes independence through the row alias; actual payload observations should continue.

All 11 wave-19 language regressions, 14 focused CLI regressions, and three additional targeted tests passed. The broader histories run exceeded my 180-second timeout.

Cross-platform qualification remains separately pending. This source verdict does not claim qualification completion. No repository source was modified; prior reviews and `WORKSTREAM-REPORT.md` remained unread.
